```cpp
    return 0;
}
```

### 完美转发的最佳实践

1. **使用通用引用**：
   - 在模板中使用`T&&`形式的参数
   - 理解引用折叠规则

2. **正确使用std::forward**：
   - 只在模板参数推导上下文中使用
   - 与模板参数类型一起使用

3. **避免过度转发**：
   - 只在需要保持参数类型时使用
   - 简单情况可以使用值传递

4. **理解引用折叠**：
   - `T& &` → `T&`
   - `T& &&` → `T&`
   - `T&& &` → `T&`
   - `T&& &&` → `T&&`

5. **处理重载**：
   - 完美转发可能与重载冲突
   - 使用标签分发或SFINAE解决

完美转发是现代C++中强大的泛型编程技术，它允许编写高效、灵活的代码，同时保持类型安全。通过合理使用完美转发，可以简化代码结构，提高性能，并增强代码的可重用性。

## 22. 什么是C++11的noexcept？它有什么特点和用途？

**答案：**
noexcept是C++11引入的关键字，用于指定函数不会抛出异常。它是一种异常规范，取代了C++98中的动态异常规范，提供了更好的性能优化和更清晰的异常处理语义。

### noexcept的概念

noexcept用于声明函数不会抛出异常，它有两种形式：

1. **noexcept**：无条件声明函数不抛出异常
2. **noexcept(expression)**：有条件声明函数不抛出异常，取决于表达式的值

### noexcept的语法

```cpp
// 无条件noexcept
void function1() noexcept;

// 有条件noexcept
void function2() noexcept(noexcept(function1()));

// 等价形式
void function3() noexcept(true);   // 不抛出异常
void function4() noexcept(false);  // 可能抛出异常
```

### noexcept的特点

1. **编译时检查**：
   - 编译器在编译时检查noexcept规范
   - 违反noexcept规范会调用std::terminate

2. **性能优化**：
   - 允许编译器生成更高效的代码
   - 减少异常处理的开销

3. **类型系统的一部分**：
   - noexcept是函数类型的一部分
   - 影响函数重载和模板特化

4. **与移动语义结合**：
   - 移动操作通常标记为noexcept
   - 影响STL容器的行为

### noexcept的用途

1. **移动操作优化**：
   - 标记移动构造函数和移动赋值运算符
   - 使STL容器优先使用移动语义

2. **性能关键代码**：
   - 标记不抛出异常的函数
   - 减少异常处理开销

3. **API设计**：
   - 明确表示函数不会抛出异常
   - 提供更清晰的接口契约

4. **模板编程**：
   - 条件性noexcept规范
   - 基于类型特性的条件异常

### noexcept使用示例

```cpp
#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <type_traits>
#include <stdexcept>

// 演示基本noexcept用法
void basicNoexceptFunction() noexcept {
    std::cout << "This function cannot throw exceptions\n";
}

void potentiallyThrowingFunction() {
    std::cout << "This function might throw exceptions\n";
    throw std::runtime_error("Exception thrown");
}

// 条件性noexcept
template<typename T>
void conditionalNoexceptFunction(T&& arg) noexcept(noexcept(T(std::forward<T>(arg)))) {
    T temp(std::forward<T>(arg));
    std::cout << "Conditional noexcept function called\n";
}

void demonstrateBasicNoexcept() {
    std::cout << "=== Basic noexcept Usage ===\n";
    
    // 检查函数是否为noexcept
    std::cout << "basicNoexceptFunction is noexcept: " 
              << noexcept(basicNoexceptFunction()) << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "potentiallyThrowingFunction is noexcept: " 
              << noexcept(potentiallyThrowingFunction()) << "\n";
    
    // 测试条件性noexcept
    std::string str = "Hello";
    std::cout << "conditionalNoexceptFunction with string is noexcept: " 
              << noexcept(conditionalNoexceptFunction(str)) << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "conditionalNoexceptFunction with int is noexcept: " 
              << noexcept(conditionalNoexceptFunction(42)) << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示noexcept与移动语义
class NoexceptExample {
private:
    int* data_;
    size_t size_;
    
public:
    // 构造函数
    explicit NoexceptExample(size_t size = 0) : size_(size) {
        std::cout << "Constructor\n";
        data_ = new int[size_];
        for (size_t i = 0; i < size_; ++i) {
            data_[i] = static_cast<int>(i);
        }
    }
    
    // 拷贝构造函数
    NoexceptExample(const NoexceptExample& other) : size_(other.size_) {
        std::cout << "Copy constructor\n";
        data_ = new int[size_];
        for (size_t i = 0; i < size_; ++i) {
            data_[i] = other.data_[i];
        }
    }
    
    // 移动构造函数（noexcept）
    NoexceptExample(NoexceptExample&& other) noexcept : data_(other.data_), size_(other.size_) {
        std::cout << "Move constructor (noexcept)\n";
        other.data_ = nullptr;
        other.size_ = 0;
    }
    
    // 拷贝赋值运算符
    NoexceptExample& operator=(const NoexceptExample& other) {
        std::cout << "Copy assignment operator\n";
        if (this != &other) {
            delete[] data_;
            size_ = other.size_;
            data_ = new int[size_];
            for (size_t i = 0; i < size_; ++i) {
                data_[i] = other.data_[i];
            }
        }
        return *this;
    }
    
    // 移动赋值运算符（noexcept）
    NoexceptExample& operator=(NoexceptExample&& other) noexcept {
        std::cout << "Move assignment operator (noexcept)\n";
        if (this != &other) {
            delete[] data_;
            data_ = other.data_;
            size_ = other.size_;
            other.data_ = nullptr;
            other.size_ = 0;
        }
        return *this;
    }
    
    // 析构函数
    ~NoexceptExample() {
        std::cout << "Destructor\n";
        delete[] data_;
    }
    
    // 获取大小
    size_t size() const { return size_; }
};

// 非noexcept移动操作的类
class NonNoexceptExample {
private:
    int* data_;
    size_t size_;
    
public:
    // 构造函数
    explicit NonNoexceptExample(size_t size = 0) : size_(size) {
        std::cout << "NonNoexcept Constructor\n";
        data_ = new int[size_];
        for (size_t i = 0; i < size_; ++i) {
            data_[i] = static_cast<int>(i);
        }
    }
    
    // 拷贝构造函数
    NonNoexceptExample(const NonNoexceptExample& other) : size_(other.size_) {
        std::cout << "NonNoexcept Copy constructor\n";
        data_ = new int[size_];
        for (size_t i = 0; i < size_; ++i) {
            data_[i] = other.data_[i];
        }
    }
    
    // 移动构造函数（非noexcept）
    NonNoexceptExample(NonNoexceptExample&& other) : data_(other.data_), size_(other.size_) {
        std::cout << "NonNoexcept Move constructor (not noexcept)\n";
        other.data_ = nullptr;
        other.size_ = 0;
    }
    
    // 析构函数
    ~NonNoexceptExample() {
        std::cout << "NonNoexcept Destructor\n";
        delete[] data_;
    }
    
    // 获取大小
    size_t size() const { return size_; }
};

void demonstrateNoexceptWithMoveSemantics() {
    std::cout << "=== noexcept with Move Semantics ===\n";
    
    // 测试noexcept类的移动语义
    std::cout << "Testing noexcept class:\n";
    std::vector<NoexceptExample> noexceptVec;
    noexceptVec.reserve(2);  // 预留空间，避免重新分配
    
    NoexceptExample obj1(5);
    NoexceptExample obj2(10);
    
    std::cout << "Pushing objects to vector:\n";
    noexceptVec.push_back(obj1);  // 拷贝
    noexceptVec.push_back(std::move(obj2));  // 移动
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 测试非noexcept类的移动语义
    std::cout << "Testing non-noexcept class:\n";
    std::vector<NonNoexceptExample> nonNoexceptVec;
    nonNoexceptVec.reserve(2);  // 预留空间，避免重新分配
    
    NonNoexceptExample obj3(5);
    NonNoexceptExample obj4(10);
    
    std::cout << "Pushing objects to vector:\n";
    nonNoexceptVec.push_back(obj3);  // 拷贝
    nonNoexceptVec.push_back(std::move(obj4));  // 可能是拷贝，因为移动构造函数不是noexcept
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示noexcept与异常处理
void noexceptFunction() noexcept {
    std::cout << "Inside noexcept function\n";
    // 如果这里抛出异常，程序会调用std::terminate
    // throw std::runtime_error("Exception in noexcept function");
}

void throwingFunction() {
    std::cout << "Inside throwing function\n";
    throw std::runtime_error("Exception in throwing function");
}

void demonstrateNoexceptWithExceptionHandling() {
    std::cout << "=== noexcept with Exception Handling ===\n";
    
    // 正常调用noexcept函数
    noexceptFunction();
    
    // 尝试捕获异常
    try {
        throwingFunction();
    } catch (const std::exception& e) {
        std::cout << "Caught exception: " << e.what() << "\n";
    }
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示noexcept与模板编程
template<typename T>
void swapWithNoexcept(T& a, T& b) noexcept(noexcept(a.swap(b))) {
    a.swap(b);
}

class Swappable {
private:
    int value_;
    
public:
    explicit Swappable(int value = 0) : value_(value) {}
    
    // noexcept swap函数
    void swap(Swappable& other) noexcept {
        std::swap(value_, other.value_);
    }
    
    int value() const { return value_; }
};

class NonNoexceptSwappable {
private:
    int value_;
    
public:
    explicit NonNoexceptSwappable(int value = 0) : value_(value) {}
    
    // 非noexcept swap函数
    void swap(NonNoexceptSwappable& other) {
        std::swap(value_, other.value_);
        // 可能抛出异常的代码
        // throw std::runtime_error("Exception in swap");
    }
    
    int value() const { return value_; }
};

void demonstrateNoexceptWithTemplates() {
    std::cout << "=== noexcept with Templates ===\n";
    
    Swappable a(10), b(20);
    std::cout << "Before swap: a=" << a.value() << ", b=" << b.value() << "\n";
    swapWithNoexcept(a, b);
    std::cout << "After swap: a=" << a.value() << ", b=" << b.value() << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "swapWithNoexcept with Swappable is noexcept: " 
              << noexcept(swapWithNoexcept(a, b)) << "\n";
    
    NonNoexceptSwappable c(30), d(40);
    std::cout << "\nBefore swap: c=" << c.value() << ", d=" << d.value() << "\n";
    swapWithNoexcept(c, d);
    std::cout << "After swap: c=" << c.value() << ", d=" << d.value() << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "swapWithNoexcept with NonNoexceptSwappable is noexcept: " 
              << noexcept(swapWithNoexcept(c, d)) << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示noexcept与函数重载
void overloadedFunction(int) noexcept {
    std::cout << "overloadedFunction(int) noexcept\n";
}

void overloadedFunction(double) {
    std::cout << "overloadedFunction(double) may throw\n";
}

template<typename T>
void templateFunction(T&& arg) noexcept(noexcept(overloadedFunction(std::forward<T>(arg)))) {
    overloadedFunction(std::forward<T>(arg));
}

void demonstrateNoexceptWithOverloading() {
    std::cout << "=== noexcept with Overloading ===\n";
    
    int i = 42;
    double d = 3.14;
    
    std::cout << "templateFunction with int is noexcept: " 
              << noexcept(templateFunction(i)) << "\n";
    templateFunction(i);
    
    std::cout << "\ntemplateFunction with double is noexcept: " 
              << noexcept(templateFunction(d)) << "\n";
    templateFunction(d);
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示noexcept与lambda表达式
void demonstrateNoexceptWithLambdas() {
    std::cout << "=== noexcept with Lambdas ===\n";
    
    // noexcept lambda
    auto noexceptLambda = []() noexcept { 
        std::cout << "noexcept lambda\n"; 
    };
    
    // potentially throwing lambda
    auto throwingLambda = []() { 
        std::cout << "potentially throwing lambda\n"; 
        // throw std::runtime_error("Exception in lambda");
    };
    
    std::cout << "noexceptLambda is noexcept: " << noexcept(noexceptLambda()) << "\n";
    noexceptLambda();
    
    std::cout << "\nthrowingLambda is noexcept: " << noexcept(throwingLambda()) << "\n";
    throwingLambda();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示noexcept与类层次结构
class Base {
public:
    virtual void virtualFunction() noexcept {
        std::cout << "Base::virtualFunction noexcept\n";
    }
    
    virtual ~Base() = default;
};

class Derived : public Base {
public:
    // 重写函数必须保持相同的noexcept规范
    void virtualFunction() noexcept override {
        std::cout << "Derived::virtualFunction noexcept\n";
    }
};

class DerivedThrowing : public Base {
public:
    // 错误：不能放宽异常规范
    // void virtualFunction() override {  // 编译错误
    //     std::cout << "DerivedThrowing::virtualFunction may throw\n";
    // }
};

void demonstrateNoexceptWithInheritance() {
    std::cout << "=== noexcept with Inheritance ===\n";
    
    Base* base = new Base();
    Base* derived = new Derived();
    
    base->virtualFunction();
    derived->virtualFunction();
    
    std::cout << "Base::virtualFunction is noexcept: " 
              << noexcept(base->virtualFunction()) << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "Derived::virtualFunction through Base pointer is noexcept: " 
              << noexcept(derived->virtualFunction()) << "\n";
    
    delete base;
    delete derived;
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示noexcept与std::terminate
void terminateFunction() noexcept {
    std::cout << "About to throw in noexcept function\n";
    throw std::runtime_error("This will terminate the program");
}

void demonstrateNoexceptWithTerminate() {
    std::cout << "=== noexcept with std::terminate ===\n";
    
    // 注意：下面的代码会导致程序终止
    // terminateFunction();
    
    std::cout << "This function would terminate if called\n";
    std::cout << "terminateFunction is noexcept: " << noexcept(terminateFunction()) << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

int main() {
    demonstrateBasicNoexcept();
    demonstrateNoexceptWithMoveSemantics();
    demonstrateNoexceptWithExceptionHandling();
    demonstrateNoexceptWithTemplates();
    demonstrateNoexceptWithOverloading();
    demonstrateNoexceptWithLambdas();
    demonstrateNoexceptWithInheritance();
    demonstrateNoexceptWithTerminate();
    
    return 0;
}
```

### noexcept的最佳实践

1. **标记移动操作**：
   - 将移动构造函数和移动赋值运算符标记为noexcept
   - 使STL容器优先使用移动语义

2. **条件性noexcept**：
   - 使用noexcept(expression)进行条件性声明
   - 基于操作是否抛出异常决定noexcept规范

3. **API设计**：
   - 明确标记不抛出异常的函数
   - 提供清晰的接口契约

4. **性能关键代码**：
   - 在性能关键的代码路径中使用noexcept
   - 减少异常处理开销

5. **异常安全保证**：
   - noexcept提供强异常安全保证
   - 与其他异常安全级别结合使用

noexcept是现代C++中重要的异常处理机制，它不仅提供了更清晰的异常规范，还允许编译器进行更积极的优化。合理使用noexcept可以提高代码的性能和可维护性。

## 23. 什么是C++11的override和final关键字？它们有什么特点和用途？

**答案：**
override和final是C++11引入的两个新的上下文关键字，用于增强面向对象编程中的多态性和安全性。它们提供了更明确的语法来表示函数重写和类继承关系，帮助编译器捕获常见的编程错误。

### override关键字

override关键字用于明确标识派生类中的虚函数重写了基类中的虚函数。它是一个特殊的标识符，只在函数声明中有意义。

### final关键字

final关键字有两个用途：
1. 用于类声明，表示该类不能被继承
2. 用于虚函数声明，表示该虚函数不能被进一步重写

### override和final的特点

1. **上下文关键字**：
   - 只在特定上下文中才有特殊含义
   - 不会破坏现有代码（可以用作变量名等）

2. **编译时检查**：
   - 编译器在编译时验证override和final的使用
   - 帮助捕获编程错误

3. **提高代码可读性**：
   - 明确表达程序员的意图
   - 使代码更易于理解和维护

4. **防止意外行为**：
   - 防止函数签名不匹配导致的隐藏问题
   - 防止意外的重写或继承

### override和final的用途

1. **防止函数签名错误**：
   - 使用override确保函数确实重写了基类函数
   - 捕获函数签名不匹配的错误

2. **控制继承层次**：
   - 使用final防止类被继承
   - 使用final防止虚函数被重写

3. **明确设计意图**：
   - 明确表示函数是重写
   - 明确表示类或函数是最终的

4. **优化编译器行为**：
   - 帮助编译器进行更好的优化
   - 可能启用虚函数调用的去虚拟化

### override和final使用示例

```cpp
#include <iostream>
#include <memory>
#include <vector>

// 演示override关键字
class Base {
public:
    virtual void virtualFunction() {
        std::cout << "Base::virtualFunction()\n";
    }
    
    virtual void virtualFunctionWithParams(int x, double y) {
        std::cout << "Base::virtualFunctionWithParams(int, double)\n";
    }
    
    virtual void constVirtualFunction() const {
        std::cout << "Base::constVirtualFunction()\n";
    }
    
    virtual ~Base() = default;
};

// 正确使用override
class CorrectDerived : public Base {
public:
    void virtualFunction() override {
        std::cout << "CorrectDerived::virtualFunction()\n";
    }
    
    void virtualFunctionWithParams(int x, double y) override {
        std::cout << "CorrectDerived::virtualFunctionWithParams(int, double)\n";
    }
    
    void constVirtualFunction() const override {
        std::cout << "CorrectDerived::constVirtualFunction()\n";
    }
};

// 错误使用override的例子（注释掉以避免编译错误）
/*
class IncorrectDerived : public Base {
public:
    // 错误：函数签名不匹配，参数类型不同
    void virtualFunctionWithParams(double x, int y) override {
        std::cout << "IncorrectDerived::virtualFunctionWithParams(double, int)\n";
    }
    
    // 错误：基类中没有对应的虚函数
    void nonExistentFunction() override {
        std::cout << "IncorrectDerived::nonExistentFunction()\n";
    }
    
    // 错误：缺少const限定符
    void constVirtualFunction() override {
        std::cout << "IncorrectDerived::constVirtualFunction()\n";
    }
};
*/

// 演示final关键字用于类
class FinalClass final {
public:
    void regularFunction() {
        std::cout << "FinalClass::regularFunction()\n";
    }
};

// 错误：不能继承final类（注释掉以避免编译错误）
/*
class DerivedFromFinal : public FinalClass {
    // 编译错误：不能继承final类
};
*/

// 演示final关键字用于虚函数
class BaseWithFinalFunction {
public:
    virtual void regularVirtualFunction() {
        std::cout << "BaseWithFinalFunction::regularVirtualFunction()\n";
    }
    
    virtual void finalVirtualFunction() final {
        std::cout << "BaseWithFinalFunction::finalVirtualFunction()\n";
    }
    
    virtual ~BaseWithFinalFunction() = default;
};

class DerivedFromBaseWithFinal : public BaseWithFinalFunction {
public:
    void regularVirtualFunction() override {
        std::cout << "DerivedFromBaseWithFinal::regularVirtualFunction()\n";
    }
    
    // 错误：不能重写final虚函数（注释掉以避免编译错误）
    /*
    void finalVirtualFunction() override {
        std::cout << "DerivedFromBaseWithFinal::finalVirtualFunction()\n";
    }
    */
};

// 演示override和final的组合使用
class BaseForOverrideFinal {
public:
    virtual void virtualFunction() {
        std::cout << "BaseForOverrideFinal::virtualFunction()\n";
    }
    
    virtual ~BaseForOverrideFinal() = default;
};

class IntermediateClass : public BaseForOverrideFinal {
public:
    void virtualFunction() override final {
        std::cout << "IntermediateClass::virtualFunction() (final)\n";
    }
};

// 错误：不能重写final虚函数（注释掉以避免编译错误）
/*
class FinalDerived : public IntermediateClass {
public:
    void virtualFunction() override {
        std::cout << "FinalDerived::virtualFunction()\n";
    }
};
*/

void demonstrateOverrideAndFinal() {
    std::cout << "=== Override and Final Demonstration ===\n";
    
    // 演示override
    std::cout << "Demonstrating override:\n";
    Base* basePtr = new CorrectDerived();
    basePtr->virtualFunction();
    basePtr->virtualFunctionWithParams(10, 3.14);
    basePtr->constVirtualFunction();
    delete basePtr;
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 演示final类
    std::cout << "Demonstrating final class:\n";
    FinalClass finalObj;
    finalObj.regularFunction();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 演示final虚函数
    std::cout << "Demonstrating final virtual function:\n";
    BaseWithFinalFunction* baseFinalPtr = new DerivedFromBaseWithFinal();
    baseFinalPtr->regularVirtualFunction();
    baseFinalPtr->finalVirtualFunction();
    delete baseFinalPtr;
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 演示override和final的组合
    std::cout << "Demonstrating override and final combination:\n";
    BaseForOverrideFinal* baseOverrideFinalPtr = new IntermediateClass();
    baseOverrideFinalPtr->virtualFunction();
    delete baseOverrideFinalPtr;
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示override和final在复杂继承层次中的应用
class Animal {
public:
    virtual void makeSound() {
        std::cout << "Animal makes a sound\n";
    }
    
    virtual void eat() {
        std::cout << "Animal eats\n";
    }
    
    virtual ~Animal() = default;
};

class Mammal : public Animal {
public:
    void makeSound() override {
        std::cout << "Mammal makes a sound\n";
    }
    
    void breathe() {
        std::cout << "Mammal breathes air\n";
    }
};

class Bird : public Animal {
public:
    void makeSound() override {
        std::cout << "Bird makes a sound\n";
    }
    
    void fly() {
        std::cout << "Bird flies\n";
    }
};

class Dog final : public Mammal {
public:
    void makeSound() override final {
        std::cout << "Dog barks\n";
    }
    
    void wagTail() {
        std::cout << "Dog wags tail\n";
    }
};

class Cat final : public Mammal {
public:
    void makeSound() override final {
        std::cout << "Cat meows\n";
    }
    
    void purr() {
        std::cout << "Cat purrs\n";
    }
};

// 错误：不能继承final类（注释掉以避免编译错误）
/*
class Puppy : public Dog {
public:
    void makeSound() override {
        std::cout << "Puppy yips\n";
    }
};
*/

class Eagle final : public Bird {
public:
    void makeSound() override final {
        std::cout << "Eagle screeches\n";
    }
    
    void hunt() {
        std::cout << "Eagle hunts prey\n";
    }
};

void demonstrateComplexInheritance() {
    std::cout << "=== Complex Inheritance with Override and Final ===\n";
    
    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<Animal>> animals;
    
    animals.push_back(std::make_unique<Dog>());
    animals.push_back(std::make_unique<Cat>());
    animals.push_back(std::make_unique<Eagle>());
    
    for (const auto& animal : animals) {
        animal->makeSound();
        animal->eat();
    }
    
    // 演示特定类的方法
    Dog dog;
    dog.makeSound();
    dog.wagTail();
    dog.breathe();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    Cat cat;
    cat.makeSound();
    cat.purr();
    cat.breathe();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    Eagle eagle;
    eagle.makeSound();
    eagle.fly();
    eagle.hunt();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示override和final在模板中的应用
template<typename Base>
class TemplateDerived : public Base {
public:
    void makeSound() override {
        std::cout << "TemplateDerived makes a sound\n";
    }
};

void demonstrateTemplateWithOverride() {
    std::cout << "=== Template with Override ===\n";
    
    TemplateDerived<Mammal> templateMammal;
    templateMammal.makeSound();
    templateMammal.eat();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示override和final与多态性的关系
class Shape {
public:
    virtual double area() const = 0;
    virtual double perimeter() const = 0;
    virtual void draw() const {
        std::cout << "Drawing a generic shape\n";
    }
    
    virtual ~Shape() = default;
};

class Circle : public Shape {
private:
    double radius_;
    
public:
    explicit Circle(double radius) : radius_(radius) {}
    
    double area() const override final {
        return 3.14159 * radius_ * radius_;
    }
    
    double perimeter() const override final {
        return 2 * 3.14159 * radius_;
    }
    
    void draw() const override {
        std::cout << "Drawing a circle with radius " << radius_ << "\n";
    }
};

class Rectangle : public Shape {
private:
    double width_;
    double height_;
    
public:
    Rectangle(double width, double height) : width_(width), height_(height) {}
    
    double area() const override final {
        return width_ * height_;
    }
    
    double perimeter() const override final {
        return 2 * (width_ + height_);
    }
    
    void draw() const override {
        std::cout << "Drawing a rectangle with width " << width_ 
                  << " and height " << height_ << "\n";
    }
};

void demonstratePolymorphismWithOverrideFinal() {
    std::cout << "=== Polymorphism with Override and Final ===\n";
    
    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<Shape>> shapes;
    
    shapes.push_back(std::make_unique<Circle>(5.0));
    shapes.push_back(std::make_unique<Rectangle>(4.0, 6.0));
    
    for (const auto& shape : shapes) {
        shape->draw();
        std::cout << "Area: " << shape->area() << "\n";
        std::cout << "Perimeter: " << shape->perimeter() << "\n\n";
    }
}

int main() {
    demonstrateOverrideAndFinal();
    demonstrateComplexInheritance();
    demonstrateTemplateWithOverride();
    demonstratePolymorphismWithOverrideFinal();
    
    return 0;
}
```

### override和final的最佳实践

1. **始终使用override**：
   - 为所有重写的虚函数添加override关键字
   - 帮助编译器捕获函数签名错误

2. **合理使用final**：
   - 对于不应该被继承的类使用final
   - 对于不应该被重写的虚函数使用final

3. **文档化设计意图**：
   - 使用override和final明确表达设计意图
   - 使代码更易于理解和维护

4. **组合使用**：
   - 可以在同一个函数声明中同时使用override和final
   - 表示函数是重写且是最终的

5. **注意兼容性**：
   - override和final是C++11特性
   - 考虑与旧代码的兼容性

override和final关键字是现代C++中重要的面向对象编程增强，它们提供了更明确的语法和更好的编译时检查，有助于编写更安全、更可维护的面向对象代码。通过合理使用这些关键字，可以避免常见的多态性错误，并清晰地表达设计意图。

## 24. 什么是C++11的enum class（强类型枚举）？它与传统枚举有什么区别？

**答案：**
enum class（也称为强类型枚举）是C++11引入的新型枚举类型，它解决了传统枚举的许多问题，提供了更强的类型安全和更好的作用域控制。

### 传统枚举的问题

传统枚举（plain enum）存在以下问题：

1. **作用域污染**：枚举值被导出到外围作用域
2. **隐式转换**：可以隐式转换为整数
3. **底层类型不明确**：底层类型由编译器决定
4. **前向声明限制**：不能前向声明

### enum class的特点

1. **强类型**：不能隐式转换为整数
2. **作用域限定**：枚举值必须通过枚举类型名访问
3. **明确底层类型**：可以指定底层类型（默认为int）
4. **前向声明**：支持前向声明
5. **更好的类型安全**：防止不同枚举类型之间的比较

### enum class与传统枚举的区别

| 特性 | 传统枚举 | enum class |
|------|----------|------------|
| 作用域 | 全局作用域 | 枚举类型作用域 |
| 隐式转换 | 可以转换为整数 | 不能隐式转换 |
| 底层类型 | 实现定义 | 可指定，默认int |
| 前向声明 | 不支持 | 支持 |
| 类型安全 | 较弱 | 强 |

### enum class使用示例

```cpp
#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <type_traits>

// 演示传统枚举的问题
enum Color {
    RED,    // 值为0
    GREEN,  // 值为1
    BLUE    // 值为2
};

// 可能与Color枚举值冲突
enum TrafficLight {
    RED,    // 与Color::RED冲突
    YELLOW,
    GREEN   // 与Color::GREEN冲突
};

// 演示enum class
enum class ColorClass : int {
    RED = 0,
    GREEN = 1,
    BLUE = 2
};

enum class TrafficLightClass : char {
    RED = 'R',
    YELLOW = 'Y',
    GREEN = 'G'
};

// 前向声明enum class
enum class Status : int;  // 前向声明

// 定义前向声明的enum
enum class Status : int {
    SUCCESS = 0,
    ERROR = 1,
    PENDING = 2
};

void demonstrateBasicEnumUsage() {
    std::cout << "=== Basic Enum Usage ===\n";
    
    // 传统枚举
    Color c = RED;
    std::cout << "Traditional enum value: " << c << "\n";
    
    // 隐式转换为整数
    int colorValue = GREEN;
    std::cout << "Implicit conversion to int: " << colorValue << "\n";
    
    // 比较不同枚举类型（不安全）
    if (c == 0) {
        std::cout << "Traditional enum compared with int\n";
    }
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // enum class
    ColorClass cc = ColorClass::RED;
    // std::cout << "Enum class value: " << cc << "\n";  // 错误：不能直接输出
    
    // 显式转换为整数
    int colorClassValue = static_cast<int>(ColorClass::GREEN);
    std::cout << "Explicit conversion to int: " << colorClassValue << "\n";
    
    // 必须使用作用域限定符
    ColorClass blue = ColorClass::BLUE;
    std::cout << "Enum class with scope: " << static_cast<int>(blue) << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示枚举与函数重载
void processColor(Color color) {
    std::cout << "Processing traditional enum color: " << color << "\n";
}

void processColor(ColorClass color) {
    std::cout << "Processing enum class color: " << static_cast<int>(color) << "\n";
}

void demonstrateEnumWithFunctionOverloading() {
    std::cout << "=== Enum with Function Overloading ===\n";
    
    processColor(RED);           // 调用processColor(Color)
    processColor(ColorClass::RED);  // 调用processColor(ColorClass)
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示枚举与switch语句
void describeColor(Color color) {
    switch (color) {
        case RED:
            std::cout << "Traditional RED\n";
            break;
        case GREEN:
            std::cout << "Traditional GREEN\n";
            break;
        case BLUE:
            std::cout << "Traditional BLUE\n";
            break;
        default:
            std::cout << "Unknown color\n";
            break;
    }
}

void describeColorClass(ColorClass color) {
    switch (color) {
        case ColorClass::RED:
            std::cout << "Enum class RED\n";
            break;
        case ColorClass::GREEN:
            std::cout << "Enum class GREEN\n";
            break;
        case ColorClass::BLUE:
            std::cout << "Enum class BLUE\n";
            break;
        default:
            std::cout << "Unknown color\n";
            break;
    }
}

void demonstrateEnumWithSwitch() {
    std::cout << "=== Enum with Switch Statements ===\n";
    
    describeColor(RED);
    describeColorClass(ColorClass::GREEN);
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示枚举与容器
void demonstrateEnumWithContainers() {
    std::cout << "=== Enum with Containers ===\n";
    
    // 传统枚举容器
    std::vector<Color> colors = {RED, GREEN, BLUE};
    std::cout << "Traditional enum values: ";
    for (const auto& color : colors) {
        std::cout << color << " ";
    }
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // enum class容器
    std::vector<ColorClass> colorClasses = {ColorClass::RED, ColorClass::GREEN, ColorClass::BLUE};
    std::cout << "Enum class values: ";
    for (const auto& color : colorClasses) {
        std::cout << static_cast<int>(color) << " ";
    }
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示枚举与字符串转换
std::string colorToString(Color color) {
    switch (color) {
        case RED: return "RED";
        case GREEN: return "GREEN";
        case BLUE: return "BLUE";
        default: return "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

std::string colorClassToString(ColorClass color) {
    switch (color) {
        case ColorClass::RED: return "RED";
        case ColorClass::GREEN: return "GREEN";
        case ColorClass::BLUE: return "BLUE";
        default: return "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

Color stringToColor(const std::string& str) {
    if (str == "RED") return RED;
    if (str == "GREEN") return GREEN;
    if (str == "BLUE") return BLUE;
    return RED;  // 默认值
}

ColorClass stringToColorClass(const std::string& str) {
    if (str == "RED") return ColorClass::RED;
    if (str == "GREEN") return ColorClass::GREEN;
    if (str == "BLUE") return ColorClass::BLUE;
    return ColorClass::RED;  // 默认值
}

void demonstrateEnumStringConversion() {
    std::cout << "=== Enum String Conversion ===\n";
    
    Color c = BLUE;
    std::cout << "Traditional enum to string: " << colorToString(c) << "\n";
    
    ColorClass cc = ColorClass::BLUE;
    std::cout << "Enum class to string: " << colorClassToString(cc) << "\n";
    
    std::string redStr = "RED";
    Color redEnum = stringToColor(redStr);
    ColorClass redEnumClass = stringToColorClass(redStr);
    
    std::cout << "String to traditional enum: " << colorToString(redEnum) << "\n";
    std::cout << "String to enum class: " << colorClassToString(redEnumClass) << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示枚举与位运算
enum Permission {
    READ = 1 << 0,    // 1
    WRITE = 1 << 1,   // 2
    EXECUTE = 1 << 2  // 4
};

enum class PermissionClass : unsigned int {
    READ = 1 << 0,    // 1
    WRITE = 1 << 1,   // 2
    EXECUTE = 1 << 2  // 4
};

void demonstrateEnumWithBitOperations() {
    std::cout << "=== Enum with Bit Operations ===\n";
    
    // 传统枚举位运算
    Permission userPerm = static_cast<Permission>(READ | WRITE);
    std::cout << "User permission (traditional): " << userPerm << "\n";
    
    if (userPerm & READ) {
        std::cout << "User has read permission\n";
    }
    
    // enum class位运算（需要显式转换）
    PermissionClass adminPerm = static_cast<PermissionClass>(
        static_cast<unsigned int>(PermissionClass::READ) | 
        static_cast<unsigned int>(PermissionClass::WRITE) | 
        static_cast<unsigned int>(PermissionClass::EXECUTE)
    );
    
    std::cout << "Admin permission (enum class): " << static_cast<unsigned int>(adminPerm) << "\n";
    
    if (static_cast<unsigned int>(adminPerm) & static_cast<unsigned int>(PermissionClass::EXECUTE)) {
        std::cout << "Admin has execute permission\n";
    }
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示枚举与模板
template<typename Enum>
constexpr auto enumToInt(Enum value) -> typename std::underlying_type<Enum>::type {
    return static_cast<typename std::underlying_type<Enum>::type>(value);
}

template<typename Enum>
Enum intToEnum(typename std::underlying_type<Enum>::type value) {
    return static_cast<Enum>(value);
}

void demonstrateEnumWithTemplates() {
    std::cout << "=== Enum with Templates ===\n";
    
    // 传统枚举
    Color c = GREEN;
    int colorInt = enumToInt(c);
    std::cout << "Traditional enum to int using template: " << colorInt << "\n";
    
    Color colorFromInt = intToEnum<Color>(1);
    std::cout << "Int to traditional enum using template: " << colorFromInt << "\n";
    
    // enum class
    ColorClass cc = ColorClass::GREEN;
    int colorClassInt = enumToInt(cc);
    std::cout << "Enum class to int using template: " << colorClassInt << "\n";
    
    ColorClass colorClassFromInt = intToEnum<ColorClass>(1);
    std::cout << "Int to enum class using template: " << static_cast<int>(colorClassFromInt) << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示枚举与类
class TrafficLightController {
private:
    TrafficLightClass currentState_;
    
public:
    explicit TrafficLightController(TrafficLightClass initialState = TrafficLightClass::RED) 
        : currentState_(initialState) {}
    
    void changeState() {
        switch (currentState_) {
            case TrafficLightClass::RED:
                currentState_ = TrafficLightClass::GREEN;
                break;
            case TrafficLightClass::GREEN:
                currentState_ = TrafficLightClass::YELLOW;
                break;
            case TrafficLightClass::YELLOW:
                currentState_ = TrafficLightClass::RED;
                break;
        }
    }
    
    TrafficLightClass getState() const { return currentState_; }
    
    std::string getStateString() const {
        switch (currentState_) {
            case TrafficLightClass::RED: return "RED";
            case TrafficLightClass::YELLOW: return "YELLOW";
            case TrafficLightClass::GREEN: return "GREEN";
            default: return "UNKNOWN";
        }
    }
};

void demonstrateEnumInClass() {
    std::cout << "=== Enum in Class ===\n";
    
    TrafficLightController controller(TrafficLightClass::RED);
    
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        std::cout << "Current state: " << controller.getStateString() << "\n";
        controller.changeState();
    }
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示枚举与运算符重载
enum class Day : int {
    MONDAY = 0,
    TUESDAY,
    WEDNESDAY,
    THURSDAY,
    FRIDAY,
    SATURDAY,
    SUNDAY
};

// 前置递增运算符
Day& operator++(Day& day) {
    day = static_cast<Day>((static_cast<int>(day) + 1) % 7);
    return day;
}

// 后置递增运算符
Day operator++(Day& day, int) {
    Day temp = day;
    ++day;
    return temp;
}

// 输出运算符
std::ostream& operator<<(std::ostream& os, Day day) {
    switch (day) {
        case Day::MONDAY: os << "MONDAY"; break;
        case Day::TUESDAY: os << "TUESDAY"; break;
        case Day::WEDNESDAY: os << "WEDNESDAY"; break;
        case Day::THURSDAY: os << "THURSDAY"; break;
        case Day::FRIDAY: os << "FRIDAY"; break;
        case Day::SATURDAY: os << "SATURDAY"; break;
        case Day::SUNDAY: os << "SUNDAY"; break;
        default: os << "UNKNOWN"; break;
    }
    return os;
}

void demonstrateEnumOperatorOverloading() {
    std::cout << "=== Enum Operator Overloading ===\n";
    
    Day today = Day::MONDAY;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < 7; ++i) {
        std::cout << "Day " << i << ": " << today << "\n";
        today++;
    }
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示枚举与constexpr
enum class MathOperation : int {
    ADD = 0,
    SUBTRACT,
    MULTIPLY,
    DIVIDE
};

constexpr int performOperation(MathOperation op, int a, int b) {
    switch (op) {
        case MathOperation::ADD: return a + b;
        case MathOperation::SUBTRACT: return a - b;
        case MathOperation::MULTIPLY: return a * b;
        case MathOperation::DIVIDE: return b != 0 ? a / b : 0;
        default: return 0;
    }
}

void demonstrateEnumWithConstexpr() {
    std::cout << "=== Enum with Constexpr ===\n";
    
    constexpr int result1 = performOperation(MathOperation::ADD, 10, 5);
    constexpr int result2 = performOperation(MathOperation::MULTIPLY, 3, 7);
    
    std::cout << "10 + 5 = " << result1 << "\n";
    std::cout << "3 * 7 = " << result2 << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

int main() {
    demonstrateBasicEnumUsage();
    demonstrateEnumWithFunctionOverloading();
    demonstrateEnumWithSwitch();
    demonstrateEnumWithContainers();
    demonstrateEnumStringConversion();
    demonstrateEnumWithBitOperations();
    demonstrateEnumWithTemplates();
    demonstrateEnumInClass();
    demonstrateEnumOperatorOverloading();
    demonstrateEnumWithConstexpr();
    
    return 0;
}
```

### enum class的最佳实践

1. **优先使用enum class**：
   - 在新代码中优先使用enum class而不是传统枚举
   - 提供更好的类型安全和作用域控制

2. **指定底层类型**：
   - 明确指定底层类型以提高兼容性
   - 考虑内存布局和序列化需求

3. **使用前向声明**：
   - 利用enum class的前向声明能力
   - 减少编译依赖

4. **提供转换函数**：
   - 为枚举提供字符串转换函数
   - 实现必要的运算符重载

5. **与constexpr结合**：
   - 使用enum class与constexpr实现编译时计算
   - 提高性能和类型安全

enum class是现代C++中重要的类型安全增强，它解决了传统枚举的许多问题，提供了更强的类型安全和更好的作用域控制。通过合理使用enum class，可以编写更安全、更清晰的代码。

## 25. 什么是C++11的constexpr？它有什么特点和用途？

**答案：**
constexpr是C++11引入的关键字，用于指定表达式或函数可以在编译时求值。它扩展了C++的常量表达式能力，使编译器能够在编译期执行更多计算，从而提高程序性能并增强类型安全。

### constexpr的概念

constexpr（constant expression）指定一个表达式或函数可以在编译时求值。它是一种比const更强大的常量表达机制，不仅表示值不可变，还表示值可以在编译期确定。

### constexpr的特点

1. **编译时求值**：
   - constexpr表达式在编译时求值
   - 结果可以作为常量使用

2. **更严格的约束**：
   - constexpr函数必须有简单的实现
   - 不能包含非常量操作

3. **多用途性**：
   - 可用于变量、函数、构造函数
   - 可用于模板参数

4. **C++14扩展**：
   - C++14放宽了constexpr函数的限制
   - 允许局部变量、循环和条件语句

### constexpr与const的区别

| 特性 | const | constexpr |
|------|-------|-----------|
| 初始化 | 运行时或编译时 | 必须编译时 |
| 函数应用 | 不能用于函数 | 可用于函数 |
| 模板参数 | 不能作为模板参数 | 可作为模板参数 |
| 数组大小 | 不能指定数组大小 | 可指定数组大小 |
| 限制 | 较少 | 较多 |

### constexpr的用途

1. **编译时计算**：
   - 在编译时执行计算
   - 减少运行时开销

2. **常量表达式**：
   - 定义真正的常量
   - 用于需要常量表达式的上下文

3. **模板参数**：
   - 作为模板参数使用
   - 实现模板元编程

4. **优化提示**：
   - 向编译器提供优化提示
   - 可能启用更多优化

### constexpr使用示例

```cpp
#include <iostream>
#include <array>
#include <string>
#include <type_traits>

// 演示基本constexpr变量
constexpr int square(int x) {
    return x * x;
}

constexpr int factorial(int n) {
    return n <= 1 ? 1 : n * factorial(n - 1);
}

// constexpr变量
constexpr int value = 42;
constexpr int squared = square(5);  // 编译时计算
constexpr int fact5 = factorial(5);  // 编译时计算

void demonstrateBasicConstexpr() {
    std::cout << "=== Basic Constexpr ===\n";
    
    std::cout << "value: " << value << "\n";
    std::cout << "square(5): " << squared << "\n";
    std::cout << "factorial(5): " << fact5 << "\n";
    
    // constexpr变量可以作为数组大小
    int arr[squared];  // 合法，因为squared是常量表达式
    std::cout << "Array size: " << sizeof(arr) / sizeof(arr[0]) << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示constexpr与条件表达式
constexpr bool isEven(int n) {
    return n % 2 == 0;
}

constexpr int abs(int x) {
    return x < 0 ? -x : x;
}

void demonstrateConstexprWithConditionals() {
    std::cout << "=== Constexpr with Conditionals ===\n";
    
    constexpr bool even = isEven(10);
    constexpr bool odd = isEven(7);
    constexpr int absolute = abs(-5);
    
    std::cout << "isEven(10): " << even << "\n";
    std::cout << "isEven(7): " << odd << "\n";
    std::cout << "abs(-5): " << absolute << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示constexpr与数组
constexpr int sumArray(const int arr[], int size) {
    int sum = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
        sum += arr[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

void demonstrateConstexprWithArrays() {
    std::cout << "=== Constexpr with Arrays ===\n";
    
    constexpr int data[] = {1, 2, 3, 4, 5};
    constexpr int sum = sumArray(data, 5);
    
    std::cout << "Sum of array: " << sum << "\n";
    
    // 使用std::array与constexpr
    constexpr std::array<int, 5> stdArray = {1, 2, 3, 4, 5};
    
    std::cout << "std::array size: " << stdArray.size() << "\n";
    std::cout << "std::array[2]: " << stdArray[2] << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示constexpr与类
class Point {
private:
    double x_;
    double y_;
    
public:
    constexpr Point(double x, double y) : x_(x), y_(y) {}
    
    constexpr double x() const { return x_; }
    constexpr double y() const { return y_; }
    
    constexpr Point translate(double dx, double dy) const {
        return Point(x_ + dx, y_ + dy);
    }
    
    constexpr double distanceFromOrigin() const {
        return x_ * x_ + y_ * y_;
    }
};

void demonstrateConstexprWithClasses() {
    std::cout << "=== Constexpr with Classes ===\n";
    
    constexpr Point p(3.0, 4.0);
    constexpr double x = p.x();
    constexpr double y = p.y();
    constexpr double distanceSquared = p.distanceFromOrigin();
    
    std::cout << "Point: (" << x << ", " << y << ")\n";
    std::cout << "Distance squared from origin: " << distanceSquared << "\n";
    
    constexpr Point translated = p.translate(1.0, 2.0);
    std::cout << "Translated point: (" << translated.x() 
              << ", " << translated.y() << ")\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示constexpr与字符串（C++17）
#if __cplusplus >= 201703L
constexpr int stringLength(const char* str) {
    int length = 0;
    while (str[length] != '\0') {
        ++length;
    }
    return length;
}

void demonstrateConstexprWithStrings() {
    std::cout << "=== Constexpr with Strings (C++17) ===\n";
    
    constexpr int len = stringLength("Hello, constexpr!");
    std::cout << "Length of string: " << len << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}
#endif

// 演示constexpr与模板
template<int N>
constexpr int fibonacci() {
    if constexpr (N <= 1) {
        return N;
    } else {
        return fibonacci<N-1>() + fibonacci<N-2>();
    }
}

template<typename T, int N>
constexpr T power(T base) {
    if constexpr (N == 0) {
        return static_cast<T>(1);
    } else if constexpr (N % 2 == 0) {
        T half = power<T, N/2>(base);
        return half * half;
    } else {
        return base * power<T, N-1>(base);
    }
}

void demonstrateConstexprWithTemplates() {
    std::cout << "=== Constexpr with Templates ===\n";
    
    constexpr int fib10 = fibonacci<10>();
    constexpr int pow2to10 = power<int, 10>(2);
    constexpr double pow3to5 = power<double, 5>(3.0);
    
    std::cout << "Fibonacci(10): " << fib10 << "\n";
    std::cout << "2^10: " << pow2to10 << "\n";
    std::cout << "3.0^5: " << pow3to5 << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示constexpr与递归
constexpr int gcd(int a, int b) {
    return b == 0 ? a : gcd(b, a % b);
}

constexpr int binarySearch(int arr[], int size, int target) {
    int left = 0;
    int right = size - 1;
    
    while (left <= right) {
        int mid = left + (right - left) / 2;
        if (arr[mid] == target) {
            return mid;
        } else if (arr[mid] < target) {
            left = mid + 1;
        } else {
            right = mid - 1;
        }
    }
    
    return -1;  // 未找到
}

void demonstrateConstexprWithRecursion() {
    std::cout << "=== Constexpr with Recursion ===\n";
    
    constexpr int gcdResult = gcd(48, 18);
    std::cout << "GCD(48, 18): " << gcdResult << "\n";
    
    constexpr int sortedArray[] = {1, 3, 5, 7, 9, 11, 13};
    constexpr int index = binarySearch(const_cast<int*>(sortedArray), 7, 7);
    std::cout << "Index of 7 in sorted array: " << index << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示constexpr与元编程
template<typename T>
struct TypeInfo {
    static constexpr bool isIntegral = std::is_integral_v<T>;
    static constexpr bool isPointer = std::is_pointer_v<T>;
    static constexpr size_t size = sizeof(T);
};

template<typename T>
constexpr bool isEven(T value) {
    static_assert(TypeInfo<T>::isIntegral, "T must be integral");
    return value % 2 == 0;
}

void demonstrateConstexprWithMetaprogramming() {
    std::cout << "=== Constexpr with Metaprogramming ===\n";
    
    std::cout << "TypeInfo<int>::isIntegral: " << TypeInfo<int>::isIntegral << "\n";
    std::cout << "TypeInfo<int>::isPointer: " << TypeInfo<int>::isPointer << "\n";
    std::cout << "TypeInfo<int>::size: " << TypeInfo<int>::size << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "TypeInfo<int*>::isIntegral: " << TypeInfo<int*>::isIntegral << "\n";
    std::cout << "TypeInfo<int*>::isPointer: " << TypeInfo<int*>::isPointer << "\n";
    std::cout << "TypeInfo<int*>::size: " << TypeInfo<int*>::size << "\n";
    
    constexpr bool even = isEven(10);
    std::cout << "isEven(10): " << even << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示constexpr与C++14扩展
#if __cplusplus >= 201402L
constexpr int sumDigits(int n) {
    int sum = 0;
    while (n > 0) {
        sum += n % 10;
        n /= 10;
    }
    return sum;
}

constexpr bool isPalindrome(int n) {
    int original = n;
    int reversed = 0;
    
    while (n > 0) {
        reversed = reversed * 10 + n % 10;
        n /= 10;
    }
    
    return original == reversed;
}

void demonstrateConstexprC++14() {
    std::cout << "=== Constexpr C++14 Extensions ===\n";
    
    constexpr int digitSum = sumDigits(12345);
    constexpr bool palindrome = isPalindrome(121);
    
    std::cout << "Sum of digits of 12345: " << digitSum << "\n";
    std::cout << "Is 121 a palindrome: " << palindrome << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}
#endif

// 演示constexpr与C++17扩展
#if __cplusplus >= 201703L
constexpr std::pair<int, int> minMax(int arr[], int size) {
    if (size == 0) {
        return {0, 0};
    }
    
    int minVal = arr[0];
    int maxVal = arr[0];
    
    for (int i = 1; i < size; ++i) {
        if (arr[i] < minVal) {
            minVal = arr[i];
        }
        if (arr[i] > maxVal) {
            maxVal = arr[i];
        }
    }
    
    return {minVal, maxVal};
}

void demonstrateConstexprC++17() {
    std::cout << "=== Constexpr C++17 Extensions ===\n";
    
    constexpr int data[] = {5, 2, 8, 1, 9, 3};
    constexpr auto [min, max] = minMax(const_cast<int*>(data), 6);
    
    std::cout << "Min value: " << min << "\n";
    std::cout << "Max value: " << max << "\n";
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}
#endif

int main() {
    demonstrateBasicConstexpr();
    demonstrateConstexprWithConditionals();
    demonstrateConstexprWithArrays();
    demonstrateConstexprWithClasses();
    
#if __cplusplus >= 201703L
    demonstrateConstexprWithStrings();
#endif
    
    demonstrateConstexprWithTemplates();
    demonstrateConstexprWithRecursion();
    demonstrateConstexprWithMetaprogramming();
    
#if __cplusplus >= 201402L
    demonstrateConstexprC++14();
#endif

#if __cplusplus >= 201703L
    demonstrateConstexprC++17();
#endif
    
    return 0;
}
```

### constexpr的最佳实践

1. **优先使用constexpr**：
   - 对于可以在编译时计算的函数使用constexpr
   - 提高性能和类型安全

2. **保持简单**：
   - constexpr函数应该保持简单
   - 避免复杂的逻辑和副作用

3. **利用C++14/17特性**：
   - 使用C++14/17扩展的constexpr功能
   - 编写更强大的编译时计算

4. **与模板结合**：
   - 结合constexpr和模板实现元编程
   - 提高代码的通用性

5. **注意限制**：
   - 了解constexpr的限制和适用场景
   - 在适当的时候使用而不是滥用

constexpr是现代C++中强大的编译时计算机制，它不仅提高了程序性能，还增强了类型安全。通过合理使用constexpr，可以编写更高效、更安全的代码，并实现更强大的模板元编程。

## 26. 什么是C++11的委托构造函数？它有什么特点和用途？

**答案：**
委托构造函数（Delegating Constructor）是C++11引入的特性，它允许一个构造函数调用同一个类的另一个构造函数来完成初始化工作。这种机制可以减少代码重复，提高代码的可维护性。

### 委托构造函数的概念

委托构造函数允许一个构造函数在其初始化列表中调用同一个类的其他构造函数。被调用的构造函数称为"目标构造函数"，调用它的构造函数称为"委托构造函数"。

### 委托构造函数的特点

1. **减少代码重复**：
   - 避免在多个构造函数中重复相同的初始化代码
   - 将公共初始化逻辑集中在一个构造函数中

2. **简化维护**：
   - 修改初始化逻辑只需在一个地方进行
   - 降低出错的可能性

3. **清晰的初始化顺序**：
   - 目标构造函数先执行
   - 委托构造函数的函数体后执行

4. **限制**：
   - 构造函数不能同时委托和初始化成员
   - 不能形成委托循环

### 委托构造函数的语法

```cpp
class ClassName {
public:
    // 目标构造函数
    ClassName(param1_type param1, param2_type param2, param3_type param3)
        : member1_(param1), member2_(param2), member3_(param3) {
        // 初始化代码
    }
    
    // 委托构造函数
    ClassName(param1_type param1, param2_type param2)
        : ClassName(param1, param2, default_value) {  // 委托调用
        // 额外的初始化代码
    }
    
    // 另一个委托构造函数
    ClassName()
        : ClassName(default_param1, default_param2) {  // 委托调用
        // 更多的初始化代码
    }
    
private:
    member1_type member1_;
    member2_type member2_;
    member3_type member3_;
};
```

### 委托构造函数使用示例

```cpp
#include <iostream>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <memory>

// 演示基本委托构造函数
class Person {
private:
    std::string name_;
    int age_;
    std::string address_;
    
public:
    // 目标构造函数
    Person(const std::string& name, int age, const std::string& address)
        : name_(name), age_(age), address_(address) {
        std::cout << "Target constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 委托构造函数 - 只提供姓名和年龄
    Person(const std::string& name, int age)
        : Person(name, age, "Unknown") {  // 委托调用
        std::cout << "Delegating constructor (name, age) called\n";
    }
    
    // 委托构造函数 - 只提供姓名
    explicit Person(const std::string& name)
        : Person(name, 0, "Unknown") {  // 委托调用
        std::cout << "Delegating constructor (name) called\n";
    }
    
    // 默认构造函数
    Person()
        : Person("Unknown", 0, "Unknown") {  // 委托调用
        std::cout << "Default constructor called\n";
    }
    
    void display() const {
        std::cout << "Name: " << name_ 
                  << ", Age: " << age_ 
                  << ", Address: " << address_ << "\n";
    }
};

void demonstrateBasicDelegatingConstructors() {
    std::cout << "=== Basic Delegating Constructors ===\n";
    
    Person p1("John Doe", 30, "123 Main St");
    p1.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    Person p2("Jane Smith", 25);
    p2.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    Person p3("Bob Johnson");
    p3.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    Person p4;
    p4.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示委托构造函数与继承
class Employee : public Person {
private:
    std::string department_;
    double salary_;
    
public:
    // 目标构造函数
    Employee(const std::string& name, int age, const std::string& address,
             const std::string& department, double salary)
        : Person(name, age, address), department_(department), salary_(salary) {
        std::cout << "Employee target constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 委托构造函数 - 不提供部门
    Employee(const std::string& name, int age, const std::string& address, double salary)
        : Employee(name, age, address, "Unassigned", salary) {  // 委托调用
        std::cout << "Employee delegating constructor (no department) called\n";
    }
    
    // 委托构造函数 - 只提供基本信息
    Employee(const std::string& name, int age, double salary)
        : Employee(name, age, "Unknown", salary) {  // 委托调用
        std::cout << "Employee delegating constructor (basic info) called\n";
    }
    
    void display() const {
        Person::display();
        std::cout << "Department: " << department_ 
                  << ", Salary: $" << salary_ << "\n";
    }
};

void demonstrateDelegatingConstructorsWithInheritance() {
    std::cout << "=== Delegating Constructors with Inheritance ===\n";
    
    Employee e1("Alice Brown", 28, "456 Oak Ave", "Engineering", 75000.0);
    e1.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    Employee e2("Charlie Wilson", 35, "789 Pine Rd", 85000.0);
    e2.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    Employee e3("Diana Miller", 32, 65000.0);
    e3.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示委托构造函数与成员对象
class ContactInfo {
private:
    std::string email_;
    std::string phone_;
    
public:
    ContactInfo(const std::string& email, const std::string& phone)
        : email_(email), phone_(phone) {
        std::cout << "ContactInfo constructor called\n";
    }
    
    const std::string& getEmail() const { return email_; }
    const std::string& getPhone() const { return phone_; }
};

class Student : public Person {
private:
    std::string studentId_;
    ContactInfo contact_;
    std::vector<std::string> courses_;
    
public:
    // 目标构造函数
    Student(const std::string& name, int age, const std::string& address,
            const std::string& studentId, const std::string& email, const std::string& phone,
            const std::vector<std::string>& courses)
        : Person(name, age, address), 
          studentId_(studentId), 
          contact_(email, phone),
          courses_(courses) {
        std::cout << "Student target constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 委托构造函数 - 不提供课程
    Student(const std::string& name, int age, const std::string& address,
            const std::string& studentId, const std::string& email, const std::string& phone)
        : Student(name, age, address, studentId, email, phone, {}) {  // 委托调用
        std::cout << "Student delegating constructor (no courses) called\n";
    }
    
    // 委托构造函数 - 使用默认联系方式
    Student(const std::string& name, int age, const std::string& address,
            const std::string& studentId, const std::vector<std::string>& courses)
        : Student(name, age, address, studentId, "student@example.com", "555-1234", courses) {  // 委托调用
        std::cout << "Student delegating constructor (default contact) called\n";
    }
    
    // 委托构造函数 - 最少信息
    Student(const std::string& name, int age, const std::string& studentId)
        : Student(name, age, "Unknown", studentId, {}) {  // 委托调用
        std::cout << "Student delegating constructor (minimal info) called\n";
    }
    
    void display() const {
        Person::display();
        std::cout << "Student ID: " << studentId_ << "\n";
        std::cout << "Email: " << contact_.getEmail() 
                  << ", Phone: " << contact_.getPhone() << "\n";
        
        if (!courses_.empty()) {
            std::cout << "Courses: ";
            for (const auto& course : courses_) {
                std::cout << course << " ";
            }
            std::cout << "\n";
        }
    }
};

void demonstrateDelegatingConstructorsWithMemberObjects() {
    std::cout << "=== Delegating Constructors with Member Objects ===\n";
    
    std::vector<std::string> courses = {"Math", "Physics", "Computer Science"};
    
    Student s1("Eve Davis", 20, "321 Elm St", "S12345", 
              "eve@example.com", "555-5678", courses);
    s1.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    Student s2("Frank Garcia", 21, "654 Maple Dr", "S67890", 
              "frank@example.com", "555-9012");
    s2.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    Student s3("Grace Lee", 19, "987 Cedar Ln", "S13579", courses);
    s3.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    Student s4("Henry Kim", 22, "S24680");
    s4.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示委托构造函数与智能指针
class DatabaseConnection {
private:
    std::string connectionString_;
    bool isConnected_;
    
public:
    DatabaseConnection(const std::string& connectionString)
        : connectionString_(connectionString), isConnected_(false) {
        std::cout << "DatabaseConnection created\n";
    }
    
    void connect() {
        isConnected_ = true;
        std::cout << "Connected to database: " << connectionString_ << "\n";
    }
    
    void disconnect() {
        isConnected_ = false;
        std::cout << "Disconnected from database\n";
    }
    
    bool isConnectionOpen() const { return isConnected_; }
    const std::string& getConnectionString() const { return connectionString_; }
};

class UserService {
private:
    std::shared_ptr<DatabaseConnection> dbConnection_;
    std::string serviceName_;
    
public:
    // 目标构造函数
    UserService(std::shared_ptr<DatabaseConnection> dbConn, const std::string& serviceName)
        : dbConnection_(dbConn), serviceName_(serviceName) {
        std::cout << "UserService target constructor called\n";
        dbConnection_->connect();
    }
    
    // 委托构造函数 - 创建默认数据库连接
    UserService(const std::string& serviceName)
        : UserService(std::make_shared<DatabaseConnection>("default_connection_string"), serviceName) {  // 委托调用
        std::cout << "UserService delegating constructor (default DB) called\n";
    }
    
    // 委托构造函数 - 使用默认服务名
    UserService(std::shared_ptr<DatabaseConnection> dbConn)
        : UserService(dbConn, "DefaultUserService") {  // 委托调用
        std::cout << "UserService delegating constructor (default name) called\n";
    }
    
    // 默认构造函数
    UserService()
        : UserService("DefaultUserService") {  // 委托调用
        std::cout << "UserService default constructor called\n";
    }
    
    ~UserService() {
        if (dbConnection_ && dbConnection_->isConnectionOpen()) {
            dbConnection_->disconnect();
        }
        std::cout << "UserService destroyed\n";
    }
    
    void performOperation() {
        if (dbConnection_ && dbConnection_->isConnectionOpen()) {
            std::cout << "Performing operation in service: " << serviceName_ << "\n";
            std::cout << "Using database: " << dbConnection_->getConnectionString() << "\n";
        } else {
            std::cout << "Cannot perform operation: database not connected\n";
        }
    }
};

void demonstrateDelegatingConstructorsWithSmartPointers() {
    std::cout << "=== Delegating Constructors with Smart Pointers ===\n";
    
    {
        UserService service1("UserService1");
        service1.performOperation();
    }
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    {
        auto customDb = std::make_shared<DatabaseConnection>("custom_connection_string");
        UserService service2(customDb, "UserService2");
        service2.performOperation();
    }
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    {
        auto customDb = std::make_shared<DatabaseConnection>("another_connection_string");
        UserService service3(customDb);
        service3.performOperation();
    }
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    {
        UserService service4;
        service4.performOperation();
    }
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示委托构造函数的错误用法
class BadExample {
private:
    int value_;
    std::string text_;
    
public:
    // 目标构造函数
    BadExample(int value, const std::string& text)
        : value_(value), text_(text) {
        std::cout << "BadExample target constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 错误：不能同时委托和初始化成员
    /*
    BadExample(int value)
        : BadExample(value, "default"), value_(value * 2) {  // 错误！
        std::cout << "This won't compile\n";
    }
    */
    
    // 正确的委托构造函数
    BadExample(int value)
        : BadExample(value * 2, "default") {  // 正确
        std::cout << "BadExample delegating constructor called\n";
    }
};

void demonstrateDelegatingConstructorPitfalls() {
    std::cout << "=== Delegating Constructor Pitfalls ===\n";
    
    BadExample example(10);
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示委托构造函数与模板
template<typename T>
class Container {
private:
    std::vector<T> data_;
    size_t capacity_;
    
public:
    // 目标构造函数
    Container(const std::vector<T>& data, size_t capacity)
        : data_(data), capacity_(capacity) {
        std::cout << "Container target constructor called\n";
        if (capacity_ < data_.size()) {
            capacity_ = data_.size();
        }
    }
    
    // 委托构造函数 - 只提供数据
    explicit Container(const std::vector<T>& data)
        : Container(data, data.size() * 2) {  // 委托调用
        std::cout << "Container delegating constructor (data only) called\n";
    }
    
    // 委托构造函数 - 提供容量
    Container(size_t capacity)
        : Container(std::vector<T>(), capacity) {  // 委托调用
        std::cout << "Container delegating constructor (capacity only) called\n";
    }
    
    // 默认构造函数
    Container()
        : Container(10) {  // 委托调用
        std::cout << "Container default constructor called\n";
    }
    
    void add(const T& item) {
        if (data_.size() < capacity_) {
            data_.push_back(item);
        } else {
            std::cout << "Container is full\n";
        }
    }
    
    void display() const {
        std::cout << "Container capacity: " << capacity_ 
                  << ", size: " << data_.size() 
                  << ", items: ";
        for (const auto& item : data_) {
            std::cout << item << " ";
        }
        std::cout << "\n";
    }
};

void demonstrateDelegatingConstructorsWithTemplates() {
    std::cout << "=== Delegating Constructors with Templates ===\n";
    
    Container<int> c1({1, 2, 3, 4, 5}, 10);
    c1.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    Container<int> c2({10, 20, 30});
    c2.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    Container<int> c3(5);
    c3.add(100);
    c3.add(200);
    c3.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    Container<int> c4;
    c4.add(1);
    c4.add(2);
    c4.add(3);
    c4.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

int main() {
    demonstrateBasicDelegatingConstructors();
    demonstrateDelegatingConstructorsWithInheritance();
    demonstrateDelegatingConstructorsWithMemberObjects();
    demonstrateDelegatingConstructorsWithSmartPointers();
    demonstrateDelegatingConstructorPitfalls();
    demonstrateDelegatingConstructorsWithTemplates();
    
    return 0;
}
```

### 委托构造函数的最佳实践

1. **选择合适的目标构造函数**：
   - 选择参数最多的构造函数作为目标构造函数
   - 确保目标构造函数完成所有必要的初始化

2. **避免委托循环**：
   - 确保委托链不会形成循环
   - 构造函数不能直接或间接委托给自己

3. **注意初始化顺序**：
   - 目标构造函数先执行
   - 委托构造函数的函数体后执行

4. **与继承结合使用**：
   - 在派生类中可以委托给基类构造函数
   - 然后在派生类中完成额外的初始化

5. **减少代码重复**：
   - 将公共初始化逻辑集中在目标构造函数中
   - 通过委托构造函数提供不同的初始化方式

委托构造函数是C++11中非常有用的特性，它可以帮助我们编写更简洁、更可维护的代码。通过合理使用委托构造函数，可以减少代码重复，降低出错的可能性，并使类的初始化逻辑更加清晰。

## 27. 什么是C++11的继承构造函数？它有什么特点和用途？

**答案：**
继承构造函数（Inheriting Constructor）是C++11引入的特性，它允许派生类直接继承基类的构造函数，而无需重新编写相同的构造函数代码。这种机制可以简化派生类的构造函数定义，特别是在多重继承的情况下。

### 继承构造函数的概念

继承构造函数允许派生类使用`using Base::Base`声明来继承基类的构造函数。这样，派生类就会自动获得与基类相同签名的构造函数，这些构造函数会直接调用对应的基类构造函数来初始化基类部分。

### 继承构造函数的特点

1. **简化代码**：
   - 避免在派生类中重复定义相同的构造函数
   - 特别适用于有多个构造函数的基类

2. **自动生成**：
   - 编译器会为每个继承的构造函数生成对应的派生类构造函数
   - 生成的构造函数参数与基类构造函数相同

3. **与多重继承兼容**：
   - 可以从多个基类继承构造函数
   - 需要注意构造函数的歧义问题

4. **限制**：
   - 继承的构造函数不会被用于拷贝/移动构造函数
   - 如果派生类有自己的成员变量，需要额外初始化

### 继承构造函数的语法

```cpp
class Base {
public:
    Base();  // 默认构造函数
    Base(int x);  // 参数化构造函数
    Base(int x, double y);  // 多参数构造函数
    Base(const Base& other);  // 拷贝构造函数
    Base(Base&& other) noexcept;  // 移动构造函数
    
    // 其他成员...
};

class Derived : public Base {
public:
    // 继承Base的所有构造函数
    using Base::Base;
    
    // 可以定义自己的构造函数
    Derived(int x, double y, const std::string& z);
    
    // 其他成员...
private:
    std::string str_;
};
```

### 继承构造函数使用示例

```cpp
#include <iostream>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <memory>

// 演示基本继承构造函数
class Shape {
protected:
    std::string name_;
    int x_, y_;  // 位置坐标
    
public:
    // 默认构造函数
    Shape() : name_("Unknown"), x_(0), y_(0) {
        std::cout << "Shape default constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 参数化构造函数
    Shape(const std::string& name, int x, int y)
        : name_(name), x_(x), y_(y) {
        std::cout << "Shape parameterized constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 拷贝构造函数
    Shape(const Shape& other)
        : name_(other.name_), x_(other.x_), y_(other.y_) {
        std::cout << "Shape copy constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 移动构造函数
    Shape(Shape&& other) noexcept
        : name_(std::move(other.name_)), x_(other.x_), y_(other.y_) {
        std::cout << "Shape move constructor called\n";
    }
    
    virtual ~Shape() = default;
    
    virtual void draw() const {
        std::cout << "Drawing shape: " << name_ 
                  << " at (" << x_ << ", " << y_ << ")\n";
    }
    
    const std::string& getName() const { return name_; }
    int getX() const { return x_; }
    int getY() const { return y_; }
};

// 使用继承构造函数的派生类
class Circle : public Shape {
private:
    double radius_;
    
public:
    // 继承Shape的所有构造函数
    using Shape::Shape;
    
    // Circle特有的构造函数
    Circle(const std::string& name, int x, int y, double radius)
        : Shape(name, x, y), radius_(radius) {
        std::cout << "Circle specific constructor called\n";
    }
    
    void draw() const override {
        std::cout << "Drawing circle: " << name_ 
                  << " at (" << x_ << ", " << y_ 
                  << ") with radius " << radius_ << "\n";
    }
    
    double getRadius() const { return radius_; }
};

void demonstrateBasicInheritingConstructors() {
    std::cout << "=== Basic Inheriting Constructors ===\n";
    
    // 使用继承的默认构造函数
    Circle c1;
    c1.draw();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 使用继承的参数化构造函数
    Circle c2("Red Circle", 10, 20);
    c2.draw();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 使用继承的拷贝构造函数
    Circle c3(c2);
    c3.draw();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 使用Circle特有的构造函数
    Circle c4("Blue Circle", 30, 40, 5.5);
    c4.draw();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示继承构造函数与多重继承
class Color {
protected:
    std::string color_;
    
public:
    // 默认构造函数
    Color() : color_("Black") {
        std::cout << "Color default constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 参数化构造函数
    explicit Color(const std::string& color) : color_(color) {
        std::cout << "Color parameterized constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 拷贝构造函数
    Color(const Color& other) : color_(other.color_) {
        std::cout << "Color copy constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 移动构造函数
    Color(Color&& other) noexcept : color_(std::move(other.color_)) {
        std::cout << "Color move constructor called\n";
    }
    
    virtual ~Color() = default;
    
    const std::string& getColor() const { return color_; }
    void setColor(const std::string& color) { color_ = color; }
};

// 多重继承并使用继承构造函数
class ColoredCircle : public Shape, public Color {
private:
    double radius_;
    
public:
    // 继承Shape的构造函数
    using Shape::Shape;
    
    // 继承Color的构造函数
    using Color::Color;
    
    // ColoredCircle特有的构造函数
    ColoredCircle(const std::string& name, int x, int y, 
                  const std::string& color, double radius)
        : Shape(name, x, y), Color(color), radius_(radius) {
        std::cout << "ColoredCircle specific constructor called\n";
    }
    
    void draw() const override {
        std::cout << "Drawing colored circle: " << name_ 
                  << " at (" << x_ << ", " << y_ 
                  << ") with color " << color_
                  << " and radius " << radius_ << "\n";
    }
    
    double getRadius() const { return radius_; }
};

void demonstrateInheritingConstructorsWithMultipleInheritance() {
    std::cout << "=== Inheriting Constructors with Multiple Inheritance ===\n";
    
    // 使用继承的Shape构造函数
    ColoredCircle cc1("Green Circle", 50, 60);
    cc1.draw();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 使用继承的Color构造函数
    ColoredCircle cc2(Color("Yellow"));
    cc2.draw();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 使用ColoredCircle特有的构造函数
    ColoredCircle cc3("Purple Circle", 70, 80, "Purple", 7.5);
    cc3.draw();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示继承构造函数与成员初始化
class Employee {
protected:
    std::string name_;
    int id_;
    double salary_;
    
public:
    // 默认构造函数
    Employee() : name_("Unknown"), id_(0), salary_(0.0) {
        std::cout << "Employee default constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 参数化构造函数
    Employee(const std::string& name, int id, double salary)
        : name_(name), id_(id), salary_(salary) {
        std::cout << "Employee parameterized constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 拷贝构造函数
    Employee(const Employee& other)
        : name_(other.name_), id_(other.id_), salary_(other.salary_) {
        std::cout << "Employee copy constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 移动构造函数
    Employee(Employee&& other) noexcept
        : name_(std::move(other.name_)), id_(other.id_), salary_(other.salary_) {
        std::cout << "Employee move constructor called\n";
    }
    
    virtual ~Employee() = default;
    
    virtual void display() const {
        std::cout << "Employee: " << name_ 
                  << ", ID: " << id_ 
                  << ", Salary: $" << salary_ << "\n";
    }
    
    const std::string& getName() const { return name_; }
    int getId() const { return id_; }
    double getSalary() const { return salary_; }
};

// 派生类有自己的成员变量
class Manager : public Employee {
private:
    std::string department_;
    std::vector<std::string> subordinates_;
    
public:
    // 继承Employee的所有构造函数
    using Employee::Employee;
    
    // Manager特有的构造函数
    Manager(const std::string& name, int id, double salary, 
            const std::string& department)
        : Employee(name, id, salary), department_(department) {
        std::cout << "Manager specific constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 可以在继承构造函数后添加额外的初始化
    Manager(const Employee& emp, const std::string& department)
        : Employee(emp), department_(department) {
        std::cout << "Manager constructor from Employee called\n";
    }
    
    void display() const override {
        Employee::display();
        std::cout << "Department: " << department_ << "\n";
        if (!subordinates_.empty()) {
            std::cout << "Subordinates: ";
            for (const auto& sub : subordinates_) {
                std::cout << sub << " ";
            }
            std::cout << "\n";
        }
    }
    
    void addSubordinate(const std::string& name) {
        subordinates_.push_back(name);
    }
    
    const std::string& getDepartment() const { return department_; }
};

void demonstrateInheritingConstructorsWithMemberInitialization() {
    std::cout << "=== Inheriting Constructors with Member Initialization ===\n";
    
    // 使用继承的默认构造函数
    Manager m1;
    m1.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 使用继承的参数化构造函数
    Manager m2("John Smith", 1001, 75000.0);
    m2.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 使用继承的拷贝构造函数
    Manager m3(m2);
    m3.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 使用Manager特有的构造函数
    Manager m4("Alice Johnson", 1002, 85000.0, "Engineering");
    m4.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 使用从Employee构造的Manager构造函数
    Employee emp("Bob Brown", 1003, 65000.0);
    Manager m5(emp, "Marketing");
    m5.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示继承构造函数的歧义问题
class Base1 {
public:
    Base1(int x) { std::cout << "Base1(int) called\n"; }
    Base1(double x) { std::cout << "Base1(double) called\n"; }
};

class Base2 {
public:
    Base2(int x) { std::cout << "Base2(int) called\n"; }
    Base2(const std::string& s) { std::cout << "Base2(string) called\n"; }
};

// 可能产生歧义的派生类
class AmbiguousDerived : public Base1, public Base2 {
public:
    // 继承两个基类的构造函数
    using Base1::Base1;
    using Base2::Base2;
    
    // 需要解决int参数的歧义
    AmbiguousDerived(int x) : Base1(x), Base2(x) {
        std::cout << "AmbiguousDerived(int) called\n";
    }
};

void demonstrateInheritingConstructorsAmbiguity() {
    std::cout << "=== Inheriting Constructors Ambiguity ===\n";
    
    // 使用double参数，明确调用Base1的构造函数
    AmbiguousDerived ad1(3.14);
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 使用string参数，明确调用Base2的构造函数
    AmbiguousDerived ad2(std::string("Hello"));
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 使用int参数，调用派生类定义的构造函数
    AmbiguousDerived ad3(42);
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示继承构造函数与模板
template<typename T>
class Container {
protected:
    std::vector<T> data_;
    size_t capacity_;
    
public:
    // 默认构造函数
    Container() : capacity_(10) {
        std::cout << "Container default constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 容量构造函数
    explicit Container(size_t capacity) : capacity_(capacity) {
        std::cout << "Container capacity constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 初始化列表构造函数
    Container(std::initializer_list<T> init) : data_(init), capacity_(init.size() * 2) {
        std::cout << "Container initializer list constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 拷贝构造函数
    Container(const Container& other) : data_(other.data_), capacity_(other.capacity_) {
        std::cout << "Container copy constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 移动构造函数
    Container(Container&& other) noexcept
        : data_(std::move(other.data_)), capacity_(other.capacity_) {
        std::cout << "Container move constructor called\n";
    }
    
    virtual ~Container() = default;
    
    void add(const T& item) {
        if (data_.size() < capacity_) {
            data_.push_back(item);
        }
    }
    
    void display() const {
        std::cout << "Container capacity: " << capacity_ 
                  << ", size: " << data_.size() 
                  << ", items: ";
        for (const auto& item : data_) {
            std::cout << item << " ";
        }
        std::cout << "\n";
    }
};

// 派生类模板继承构造函数
template<typename T>
class Stack : public Container<T> {
public:
    // 继承Container的所有构造函数
    using Container<T>::Container;
    
    // Stack特有的方法
    void push(const T& item) {
        this->add(item);
    }
    
    void pop() {
        if (!this->data_.empty()) {
            this->data_.pop_back();
        }
    }
    
    T& top() {
        return this->data_.back();
    }
    
    const T& top() const {
        return this->data_.back();
    }
    
    bool empty() const {
        return this->data_.empty();
    }
    
    void display() const {
        std::cout << "Stack (top to bottom): ";
        for (auto it = this->data_.rbegin(); it != this->data_.rend(); ++it) {
            std::cout << *it << " ";
        }
        std::cout << "\n";
    }
};

void demonstrateInheritingConstructorsWithTemplates() {
    std::cout << "=== Inheriting Constructors with Templates ===\n";
    
    // 使用继承的默认构造函数
    Stack<int> s1;
    s1.push(10);
    s1.push(20);
    s1.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 使用继承的容量构造函数
    Stack<int> s2(5);
    s2.push(1);
    s2.push(2);
    s2.push(3);
    s2.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 使用继承的初始化列表构造函数
    Stack<int> s3({100, 200, 300, 400});
    s3.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 使用继承的拷贝构造函数
    Stack<int> s4(s3);
    s4.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

int main() {
    demonstrateBasicInheritingConstructors();
    demonstrateInheritingConstructorsWithMultipleInheritance();
    demonstrateInheritingConstructorsWithMemberInitialization();
    demonstrateInheritingConstructorsAmbiguity();
    demonstrateInheritingConstructorsWithTemplates();
    
    return 0;
}
```

### 继承构造函数的最佳实践

1. **适当使用继承构造函数**：
   - 当派生类不需要修改基类构造函数的行为时使用
   - 避免在派生类中重复相同的构造函数代码

2. **处理歧义问题**：
   - 在多重继承中，注意构造函数的歧义
   - 必要时在派生类中显式定义构造函数来解决歧义

3. **与成员初始化结合**：
   - 继承构造函数后，可以在派生类构造函数中初始化自己的成员
   - 使用委托构造函数与继承构造函数结合

4. **注意拷贝/移动构造函数**：
   - 继承构造函数不会自动处理拷贝/移动构造函数
   - 必要时显式定义派生类的拷贝/移动构造函数

5. **与模板结合**：
   - 在类模板中使用继承构造函数可以提高代码复用
   - 注意模板参数的传递和推导

继承构造函数是C++11中非常有用的特性，它可以帮助我们简化派生类的构造函数定义，特别是在多重继承和模板类的情况下。通过合理使用继承构造函数，可以减少代码重复，提高代码的可维护性。

## 28. 什么是C++11的成员初始化器？它有什么特点和用途？

**答案：**
成员初始化器（Member Initializer）是C++11引入的特性，它允许在类定义中直接为非静态成员变量提供初始值。这种机制可以简化构造函数的定义，减少代码重复，并使成员变量的默认值更加明确。

### 成员初始化器的概念

成员初始化器允许在类定义中为非静态成员变量指定初始值，这些初始值会在所有构造函数执行之前被应用。如果在构造函数中没有显式初始化某个成员变量，那么将使用在类定义中指定的默认值。

### 成员初始化器的特点

1. **简化构造函数**：
   - 减少在构造函数中重复初始化相同的成员变量
   - 特别适用于有多个构造函数的类

2. **明确的默认值**：
   - 在类定义中直接指定成员变量的默认值
   - 使代码更加清晰和易于维护

3. **初始化顺序**：
   - 成员初始化器在构造函数初始化列表之前执行
   - 如果构造函数中有显式初始化，会覆盖成员初始化器的值

4. **适用性**：
   - 适用于非静态成员变量
   - 不适用于静态成员变量（静态成员变量需要在类外初始化）

### 成员初始化器的语法

```cpp
class ClassName {
private:
    int value_ = 10;  // 成员初始化器
    std::string text_ = "Default";  // 成员初始化器
    std::vector<int> data_{1, 2, 3, 4, 5};  // 成员初始化器
    double* ptr_ = nullptr;  // 成员初始化器
    
public:
    // 构造函数可以选择覆盖成员初始化器的值
    ClassName() {
        // value_ 将是 10，text_ 将是 "Default"
        // data_ 将是 {1, 2, 3, 4, 5}，ptr_ 将是 nullptr
    }
    
    ClassName(int value, const std::string& text)
        : value_(value), text_(text) {
        // value_ 和 text_ 被构造函数初始化列表覆盖
        // data_ 仍然是 {1, 2, 3, 4, 5}，ptr_ 仍然是 nullptr
    }
};
```

### 成员初始化器使用示例

```cpp
#include <iostream>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <memory>
#include <array>

// 演示基本成员初始化器
class Person {
private:
    std::string name_ = "Unknown";  // 默认姓名
    int age_ = 0;  // 默认年龄
    std::string address_ = "Unknown";  // 默认地址
    bool isActive_ = true;  // 默认激活状态
    
public:
    // 默认构造函数，使用所有成员初始化器的值
    Person() {
        std::cout << "Person default constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 只提供姓名的构造函数
    explicit Person(const std::string& name) : name_(name) {
        std::cout << "Person(name) constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 提供姓名和年龄的构造函数
    Person(const std::string& name, int age) : name_(name), age_(age) {
        std::cout << "Person(name, age) constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 提供所有信息的构造函数
    Person(const std::string& name, int age, const std::string& address, bool isActive)
        : name_(name), age_(age), address_(address), isActive_(isActive) {
        std::cout << "Person(name, age, address, isActive) constructor called\n";
    }
    
    void display() const {
        std::cout << "Name: " << name_ 
                  << ", Age: " << age_ 
                  << ", Address: " << address_
                  << ", Active: " << (isActive_ ? "Yes" : "No") << "\n";
    }
};

void demonstrateBasicMemberInitializers() {
    std::cout << "=== Basic Member Initializers ===\n";
    
    // 使用默认构造函数，所有成员使用成员初始化器的值
    Person p1;
    p1.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 只提供姓名，其他成员使用成员初始化器的值
    Person p2("John Doe");
    p2.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 提供姓名和年龄，其他成员使用成员初始化器的值
    Person p3("Jane Smith", 25);
    p3.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 提供所有信息，覆盖所有成员初始化器的值
    Person p4("Bob Johnson", 30, "123 Main St", false);
    p4.display();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示成员初始化器与容器
class DataProcessor {
private:
    std::vector<int> data_ = {1, 2, 3, 4, 5};  // 默认数据
    std::string delimiter_ = ",";  // 默认分隔符
    size_t maxItems_ = 100;  // 默认最大项目数
    bool caseSensitive_ = false;  // 默认不区分大小写
    
public:
    // 默认构造函数
    DataProcessor() {
        std::cout << "DataProcessor default constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 提供数据的构造函数
    explicit DataProcessor(const std::vector<int>& data) : data_(data) {
        std::cout << "DataProcessor(data) constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 提供数据和分隔符的构造函数
    DataProcessor(const std::vector<int>& data, const std::string& delimiter)
        : data_(data), delimiter_(delimiter) {
        std::cout << "DataProcessor(data, delimiter) constructor called\n";
    }
    
    // 提供所有参数的构造函数
    DataProcessor(const std::vector<int>& data, const std::string& delimiter, 
                  size_t maxItems, bool caseSensitive)
        : data_(data), delimiter_(delimiter), maxItems_(maxItems), caseSensitive_(caseSensitive) {
        std::cout << "DataProcessor(all parameters) constructor called\n";
    }
    
    void processData() {
        std::cout << "Processing data: ";
        for (size_t i = 0; i < data_.size(); ++i) {
            std::cout << data_[i];
            if (i < data_.size() - 1) {
                std::cout << delimiter_;
            }
        }
        std::cout << "\n";
        
        std::cout << "Max items: " << maxItems_ 
                  << ", Case sensitive: " << (caseSensitive_ ? "Yes" : "No") << "\n";
    }
};

void demonstrateMemberInitializersWithContainers() {
    std::cout << "=== Member Initializers with Containers ===\n";
    
    // 使用默认构造函数
    DataProcessor dp1;
    dp1.processData();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 提供自定义数据
    DataProcessor dp2({10, 20, 30});
    dp2.processData();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 提供数据和分隔符
    DataProcessor dp3({100, 200, 300, 400}, " | ");
    dp3.processData();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
    
    // 提供所有参数
    DataProcessor dp4({5, 10, 15, 20, 25}, " - ", 50, true);
    dp4.processData();
    
    std::cout << "\n";
}

// 演示成员初始化器与智能指针
class ResourceManager {
private:
    std::unique_ptr<int[]> buffer_ = std::make_unique<int[]>(10);  // 默认缓冲区大小
    size_t bufferSize_ = 10;  // 默认缓冲区大小
    std::shared_ptr<std::string> name_ = std::make_shared<std::string>("Unnamed");  // 默认名称
    bool autoResize_ = true;  // 默认自动调整大小
    
public:
    // 默认构造函数
    ResourceManager() {
        std::cout << "ResourceManager default constructor called\n";
        // 初始化缓冲区
        for (size_t i = 0; i < bufferSize_; ++i) {
            buffer_[i] = static_cast<int>(i);
        }
    }
    
    // 提供缓冲区大小的构造函数
    explicit ResourceManager(size_t bufferSize) 
        : buffer_(std::make_unique<int[]>(bufferSize)), bufferSize_(bufferSize) {
        std::cout << "ResourceManager(bufferSize) constructor called\n";
        // 初始化缓冲区
        for (size_t i = 0; i < bufferSize_; ++i) {
            buffer_[i] = static_cast<int>(i * 10);
        }
    }
    
    // 提供缓冲区大小和名称的构造函数
    ResourceManager(size_t bufferSize, const std::string& name)
        : buffer_(std::make_unique<int[]>(bufferSize)), 
          bufferSize_(bufferSize),
          name_(std::make_shared<std::string>(name)) {
        std::cout << "ResourceManager(bufferSize, name) constructor called\n";
        // 初始化缓冲区
        for (size_t i = 0; i < bufferSize_; ++i) {
            buffer_[i] = static_cast<int>(i * 100);
        }
    }
    
    // 提供所有参数的构造函数
    ResourceManager(size_t bufferSize, const std::string& name, bool autoResize)
        : buffer_(std::make_unique<int[]>(bufferSize)), 
          bufferSize_(bufferSize),
          name_(std::make_shared<std::string>(name)),
          autoResize_(autoResize) {
        std::cout << "ResourceManager(all parameters) constructor called\n";
        // 初始化缓冲区
        for (size_t i = 0; i < bufferSize_; ++i) {
            buffer_[i] = static_cast<int>(i * 1000);
        }
    }
    
    void display() const {
        std::cout << "Resource: " << *name_ 
                  << ", Buffer size: " << bufferSize_
                  << ", Auto resize: " << (autoResize_ ? "Yes" : "No") << "\n";
        
        std::cout << "Buffer content: ";
        for (size_t i = 0; i < bufferSize_; ++i) {
            std::cout << buffer_[i] << " ";
        }
        std::cout << "\n";
    }
};

void demonstrateMemberInitializersWithSmartPointers() {
```
